# Motocross Marketing — catalogo wiseco 2024

## Pagina 1

**_P O W E R S P O R T S C A T A L O G_**

## Pagina 2

# **_ABOUT WISECO PISTONS_**

Wiseco is a premier manufacturer of high-performance engine components, with a rich 80-year history in the performance and racing lifestyle. It all started when Clyde Wiseman began manufacturing his own big-bore marine engine pistons in his Cleveland garage to fill a void in the marketplace by building the performance pistons he needed to win races. Word quickly spread, and by 1941, Wiseman had founded Wiseco Piston to serve the needs of fellow motorsports enthusiasts who demanded high-quality, reliable performance parts.

Today, Wiseco is one of the world's leading manufacturers of high-performance engine components, with a dedication to producing pistons strictly using the forging process to ensure ultimate strength and durability. From powersports vehicles like ATVs, snowmobiles, and personal watercraft to four-wheel racing machines, Wiseco has a passion for motor sports and performance, and it shows in every product they produce.

**_WISECO PISTONS ARE FORGED AND MANUFACTURED IN MENTOR, OHIO WIS_** **_~~ECO.COM | 800.321.1364~~_**

## Pagina 4

# **_2-STROKE PISTONS_**

Wiseco’s legacy is rooted in 2-stroke piston manufacturing and performance, dating over 80 years ago back to 1941. Today, Wiseco 2-stroke forged pistons are the result of decades of experience, incorporating the latest in 2-stroke piston manufacturing and design to deliver the performance Wiseco built its name on. Wiseco 2-stroke forged pistons are available for everything from Honda to KTM to Yamaha, and many more.

**CLASSIC FORGED** _Reliable Rebuild_

- **Classic, reliable forged 2-stroke pistons covering a wide variety of powersports rebuilds**

- **• Everything from vintage motocross models to classic street to snowmobiles**

- **Refreshed performance from the track to the trail**

- **Forged material improves tensile strength over cast**

## **PRO-LITE**

## _All-Purpose Riding_

   - **Wiseco’s staple Forged 2-stroke piston**

   - **Great value for a reliable fun factor**

   - **• Refreshed performance from the track to the trail**

   - **Optimized forging design for strength optimization**

   - **Extra lightening operations for enhanced throttle response**

   - **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips included**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips included**

# **GP SERIES**

_Designed for Endurance/High RPM Applications_

- **Optimized race features for top-end power**

- **Hard anodized crown, ring groove, and pin bore for extended life**

- **• ArmorGlide® skirt coating reduces friction and operating noise for reduced wear and optimized performance**

- **Optimized forging design for strength-to-weight benefits**

- **Extra lightening operations for enhanced throttle response**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips included**

## Pagina 99

Engineered, tested and proven for high-level racing applications, Wiseco’s Racer Elite line delivers factory-level performance through industry leading innovation and experience. Racer Elite 2-stroke pistons set a new performance standard with proprietary technology and dyno-proven performance in a USA-made forged piston package.

# **RACER ELITE**

_Designed for Full-Race Applications_

- **Engineered to be the most advanced 2-Stroke piston on the market!**

- **Industry’s first 2-stroke piston to be forged with 2000-series alloy, providing greater tensile strength**

- **Optimized design for extreme conditions and racing applications**

- **ArmorFit™ skirt coating conforms to the bore for the ultimate in piston control and wear**

- **ArmorPlating® coating protects and improves performance in (3) main areas**

   - **Protects the crown from harmful detonation**

   - **Protects pin bore for decreased wear and bore deformation**

   - **Enhanced ring seal for long life and performance**

- **Improved Performance utilizing premium quality rings with enhanced dimensional control, which results in improved sealing and power increases**

**_99_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 116

# **_4 STROKE PISTONS_**

The Wiseco team has remained at the forefront of powersports performance through every era and evolution, including the development of high-performance 4-strokes. Designed for each specific engine application and built to the tightest tolerances for the ultimate combination of performance and strength, Wiseco 4-stroke pistons are available for everything from Honda to KTM to Yamaha, and many more.

## **FORGED SERIES**

_Multi-Purpose Riding and Racing_

- **A complete range of forged pistons engineered for each specific 4-stroke powersports engine**

- **Covering hundreds of applications across ATV, Dirt Bike, Street, Snowmobile, UTV, V-Twin and more in a variety of bore size and compression ratio options**

- **Forged material optimizes strength and service life**

- **Performance enhancing features varied across applicable applications to maximize durability and power**

- **Wrist pin, rings and circlips included**

When it comes to piston design, durability, and performance, it really helps having a great partner like Wiseco where we can bounce ideas off them and ask them to help us meet specific goals. It really helps having a group that’s professional and experienced at a high level like Wiseco.

## Pagina 194

# **_4-STROKE PISTONS_**

Engineered, tested and proven for high-level racing applications, Wiseco’s Racer Elite line delivers factory-level performance through industry leading innovation and experience. Racer Elite 4-stroke pistons set a new performance standard with industry-first design and dyno-proven performance.

- **Engineered to be a complete racing piston package ready to compete at the highest levels**

- **100% fully machined billet construction**

- **Dyno proven horsepower improvement along with high engine load durability**

- **Asymmetrical skirt design minimizes skirt area on minor thrust side, translating to quicker revving and improved power**

- **Ultra-flat top piston ring process and matching, precision-machined ring groove provide improved ring seal, reducing blow-by and providing optimized performance**

- **ArmorGlide® skirt coating aids in friction reduction and reduces skirt and cylinder wear**

- **DLC Coated Wrist Pin – F1 technology offering the best in friction reduction and reduced wear for the small-end, pin bore, and wrist pin**

**_194_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 210

# **_<u>ATV & UTV</u>_**

## **_2 & 4 STROKE_**

From classic, 2-stroke ATVs to modern, high-performance UTVs, Wiseco’s approach remains unchanged: develop quality, USA-made pistons that deliver unmatched durability and power you can feel.

### **ATV PISTONS**

_Performance For All Riding And Racing Types_

- **Variety of forged pistons covering classic 2-stroke and 4-stroke ATV platforms, including the popular Banshee, Blaster and TRX**

- **• Utility ATV and Sport ATV applications - covering everything from reliable, hard-working rebuilds to competitive race engines**

- **Variety of overbore sizes available**

- **Various piston series available, from Classic to Racer Elite, depending on the application to best suit your performance goals**

- **• Available in piston kits and top-end kits, depending on the application**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips always included!**

WISECO IS THE ONLY COMPANY I TRUST TO RUN IN MY RACE MACHINES. THEIR RACER ELITE PISTON HAS CARRIED MY YAMAHA YFZ450R TO BACK-TO-BACK CHAMPIONSHIPS AND MULTIPLE WINS IN MY CAREER. GNCC RACING IS IN A CLASS OF ITS OWN, AS WE COMPETE FOR 2 HOURS THROUGH THE MUD, DUST, SAND, AND MORE ON LONG AND GRUELING RACECOURSES. AS LONG AS MY MACHINE IS OUTFITTED WITH WISECO PRODUCTS, I KNOW THAT CHECKERED FLAG IS ALWAYS IN MY FORECAST NO MATTER THE CONDITIONS.

**_– BRYCEN NEAL, 2022 & 2023 GNCC XC1 ATV Champion_**

**_210 WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 211

# **UTV PISTONS**

_From Utility to All-Out Race!_

- **Reliable forged pistons developed specifically for a wide variety of engines and use applications**

- **Pistons to get utility vehicles back up and running reliably, ready for the hours of hard work ahead!**

- **Pistons developed and designed for modern, high-performance sport UTV engines**

   - **Various compression ratios available - covering high-revving NA builds and big-boost turbo builds!**

- **Many standard and overbore options**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips always included!**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 274

# **_STREET BIKES_**

Forged piston performance isn’t just for off-road. The high-revving, extended runtime nature of road racing and the extreme heat and cylinder pressure environments of motorcycle drag racing are perfect formulas constituting piston upgrades. Wherever your two-wheeled power goals take you, Wiseco continues to equip street motorcycle builders, racers, and enthusiasts with race-proven forged pistons.

- **Decades of dyno-tested and race-proven results!**

- **Hundreds of models covered with application-specific piston designs, from vintage classics to modern race machines**

- **Pistons available individually or as complete, multi-cylinder kits**

- **Variety of bore sizes and compression ratios to best suit your build**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips always included!**

**_274_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 318

# **_WATERCRAFT_**

Classic, 2-stroke stand-up watercraft set the tone for the high-horsepower, fuel-injected PWC machines we see on the water today. Wiseco offers a range of reliable forged pistons for each of these generations of watercraft, and everything in between!

**_318 WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 319

- **Many classic 2-stroke models covered with overbore options available off-the-shelf to make high-engine-hour rebuilds easier**

- **Tuned and tested 4-stroke options available - from riding fun to all-out race**

- **Pistons available individually or as complete, multi-cylinder kits**

- **Wrist pin, rings, and circlips always included!**

**_319_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 344

**_ENGINE REBUILD KITS FOR DIRT BIKES. ATVS & UTVS_**

***Covered under exclusive, Garage Buddy limited warranty! *Conditions  apply**

**Wiseco’s Garage Buddy line of rebuild kits helps save time and money, whether you’re working on a dirt bike, UTV, or ATV. Available for a wide variety of popular powersports applications, Garage Buddy engine rebuild kits contain everything you need to rebuild your engine from top to bottom, all in one box, under one part number. Plus, they’re covered by an exclusive, limited warranty! Get out of the garage and back on the track or trail quicker and easier with Garage Buddy.**

- **Rotating and Reciprocating Rebuild Kit - includes all top and bottom end components for a complete engine rebuild!**

- **Complete crankshaft assembly and OEMquality main bearings - designed and inspected to ensure durability**

- **All necessary gaskets, seals, and O-rings**

- **Wiseco forged piston kit(s) including necessary piston components**

- **Small-end bearing (for two-stroke motorcycle/ ATV applications) and a cam chain (for four-stroke motorcycle/ATV applications)**

- **Free Wiseco hour meter and assembly lube included to track time and maintenance and get the rebuild done right**

## Pagina 352

# **_CRANKSHAFTS AND BOTTOM END REBUILD KITS_**

Splitting the cases doesn’t always need to be a costly hassle - From individual crankshaft assemblies to bottom end rebuild kits, Wiseco has the solution for your bottom end rebuild.

## **CRANKSHAFTS**

_Thoroughly Developed, Expertly Engineered, Rigorously Tested_

- **Drop-in ready, tested and inspected complete crankshaft assembly delivering quality with value**

- **Elevated engineering and quality control measures to ensure reliable service**

- **Run-out and critical dimensions held to strict tolerances**

- **Precision machined crank webs developed and tested for ultimate strength and durability**

- **OEM quality big end bearing used in assembly**

- **Improved connecting rod process – copper plating and heat-treating optimizes material properties for improved bearing wear and maximizes durability**

- **Connecting rod surface treatment removes residual stress risers and smooths surface blends to maximize load resistance and service life**

- **Timing keyway inspected for accuracy**

- **Designs are rigorously tested for 4-hours at wide open throttle on Wiseco’s in-house engine dyno**

**_352_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 353

# **BOTTOM-END REBUILD KITS**

_Everything Needed for a Bottom End Rebuild,_

_Under One Part Number!_

- **Save time and money on your bottom end rebuild**

## Pagina 366

# **_ENGINE BEARINGS_**

With upwards of three-quarters of a million sport bikes and other powersports machines on the road, track, and trail today, replacing engine bearings can quickly become a pain point. Whether it’s fatigue from thousands of riding miles or spun bearings in highperformance builds, Wiseco’s Premium Engine Bearings were developed to fill the void of a quality, high-performance-capable replacement bearings for sport motorcycles and other applicable applications. Developed and manufactured in collaboration with the industry-leading experts at King Bearings, Wiseco’s Premium Engine Bearing line delivers significantly improved strength and wear resistance to withstand heavy engine loads and high RPMs, featuring:

- **Tri-metal construction with high-hardness intermediate layer and pMaxBlack™ overlay, ultimately delivering 20%+ more load capacity and 15%+ better fatigue resistance**

- **Availability in 5 graded sizes above and below standard and in 0.25mm increments with highly accurate wall thickness to help builders achieve exact bearing clearance without the need for grinding cranks, adjusting housing diameter, or purchasing multiple OE sets**

- **RadiaLock™ ensures optimal height values for optimized radial pressure and press fit, helping prevent bearing spinning and distortion, and improving heat transfer for improved longevity**

- **Designed, manufactured, and tested to provide improved oil wedge formation, stable hydrodynamic lubrication, minimized vibration and wear, and consistency in the high-RPM environment of sport bikes**

- **SmartLug™ design helps prevent lug-generated oil leaks by maintaining maximized load-carrying surface area**

**_366 WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 372

# **_CLUTCH KITS AND COMPONENTS_**

Wiseco clutch baskets, inner hubs, and pressure plates lead the industry in durability with maximized tensile strength thanks to in-house, USA-made forged construction. Pair that with hard-coat anodizing and Teflon coating and you have incredibly smooth modulation and extreme wear resistance.

## Pagina 386

# **_VALVETRAIN_**

## **GARAGE BUDDY STEEL VALVE KITS**

Modern 4-stroke engines’ valvetrains are highly efficient, precise systems, but when it’s time for a rebuild, replacing OEM titanium valves and other components can be costly.

- **Garage Buddy Steel Valve Kits offer a reliable solution to restore performance valvetrain operation at a fraction of the cost compared to OEM.**

- **Available in intake valve kits, exhaust valve kits, and complete valve kits for many applications**

- **Includes precision-machined, high-quality steel valves with new springs rated to match**

- **OEM-quality valve seals and free cam lube included to get the job done right!**

**_386_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 387

# **CAM CHAINS**

A must for any 4-stroke top end rebuild, cam chains are literally the connecting links from the top end to the bottom end of your engine and are critical for proper timing and engine function.

- **Manufactured in Japan using premium materials**

- **M-style link design promotes strength and longevity**

- **Designed and manufactured to exact specifications exceeding OEM standards**

# **VALVE SHIM KITS**

Easily adjust valve clearance for nearly any 4-stroke motorcycle or ATV engine! Manufactured to OEM standards

- **Shims are heat treated for long life and precision ground**

   - **to exact sizes**

- **Available in 7.48mm, 8.85mm, 9.48mm, and 10.00mm diameter sizes**

**PLUS MORE VALVETRAIN PARTS AND ACCESSORIES!**

**_387_**

**_WISECO.COM | 800.321.1364_**

## Pagina 419

# **_HOUR/TACHOMETER_**

**_One of the most critical parts of your rebuild is tracking how many hours you put on it after the engine is back together! This is why Wiseco two variations of hour meters, each of them supplied with a log book to help keep track of maintenance._**

- **_Wiseco’s standard hour meter works off inductive readings gained from your spark plug wire for an accurate and reliable reading._**

- **_These meters record up to 9,999.99 hours are not resettable or erasable._**

- **_Installation is quick and easy, especially with Wiseco’s available hour meter mounting bracket!_**

- **_Wiseco’s hour meter w/ tachometer provides all the benefits of the standard hour meter, but allows for real time tachometer readings with adjustable input, including resettable RPM compatible with single or dual-spark ignition systems!_**

## Pagina 420

# **_CYLINDER HONING PROCEDURE_**

**Cast Iron Sleeved Cylinders: To ensure proper ring seal, it is necessary to hone the cylinder with a rigid or brush hone. DO NOT install a new piston without honing the cylinder to break the glaze. Finish hone with a 280 grit. Cross hatch is necessary to ensure proper ring seal and adequate lubrication. If the cylinder is in good condition, simply honing the cylinder may be enough to restore the bore to an acceptable condition. However in some cases, boring may be necessary to insure the cylinder bore is within the tolerances outlined in the service manual.**

**Plated Cylinders: (Includes nickel ceramic coatings (Nikasil), chrome, Electrofusion, and boron composite.) If the plated cylinder is in good condition, honing may not be necessary. If deglazing is necessary, DO NOT use a ball hone. Use a rigid or brush type diamond hone. Plated cylinders cannot be bored oversize without replating or resleeving.**

**Any time a cylinder is bored or honed: All ports must be chamfered. The top of the cylinder should be deburred, and the bottom should be generously chamfered for ease of piston and ring installation. When boring a cylinder with an exhaust bridge, the face of the bridge must be relieved .002”-.004” to allow for bridge expansion. Without proper bridge clearance, engine damage could occur.**

**Exhaust Power Valves (2-Stroke engines): When boring cylinders with exhaust power valves the exhaust power valve must be inspected to ensure it does not extend into the cylinder. The exhaust power valve must have approximately .015” clearance from the cylinder bore. Without proper clearance, the exhaust power valve could contact the piston and engine damage could occur. If machining the exhaust power valve is necessary, remove metal only in the area that extends into the cylinder bore. (See illustration)**

**Remove This Material**

## **_NYLON SOFT HONE BRUSHES_**

|**BORE RANGE (MM)**|**BORE RANGE (IN)**|**PART NUMBER**|**BRUSH O.D.**|**OVERALL LENGTH**|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|**45-57mm**|**1.77" - 2.24"**|**W6075**|**2"**|**10"**|
|**57-70mm**|**2.24" - 2.76"**|**W6076**|**3"**|**10"**|
|**63-76mm**|**2.48" - 2.99"**|**W6077**|**4"**|**14"**|
|**76-89mm**|**2.99" - 3.50"**|**W6078**|**4"**|**14"**|
|**89-102mm**|**3.50" - 4.02"**|**W6079**|**4"**|**14"**|

### **IMPORTANT:**

**_After honing, the cylinder must be washed with warm soapy water to remove all honing grit. Be sure to wash away any grit that may have traveled into the transfer and exhaust ports during honing. The cylinder is NOT clean until you can wipe the cylinder wall with a clean, oil dampened cloth and it does not pick up any honing grit. Lightly oil the cylinder bore to prevent oxidation and assist with piston installation._**

## Pagina 422

# **_TAPERED RING COMPRESSOR SLEEVES_**

- Machined from Wiseco sleeve forgings to offer the same toughness as Wiseco's forged pistons.

- Hard anodized and Teflon coated for low friction and prolonged wear resistance.

- Sleeves have a smooth radius that tapers down to the specific bore size.

- Compresses the piston rings smoothly and evenly. • Greatly reduces the difficulty with installing thin high-performance oil rings.

# **_GLOSSARY: 4-STROKE RINGS_**

All Wiseco rings can be used in cast iron, nickel ceramic platings (Nikasil), boron composite, and Electrofusion bores. CAUTION: With the exception of the XJ rings, Wiseco 4-stroke rings can NOT be used in chrome plated bores. Engine damage will occur.

*XJ rings are designed to be used in chrome bores.

## Pagina 434

# **_ABOUT RWB_**

Race Winning Brands (RWB) is a leading USA based manufacturer of high- performance and racing parts for automotive and powersports aftermarkets, covering engine, transmission, electronics, and complementary components. RWB is the preferred source for high-end pistons, engine blocks, cylinder heads, connecting rods, crankshafts, clutches and other powertrainrelated performance components, plus electronic engine management and intake and exhaust systems. RWB markets its products through multiple prominent brands including BoostLine, Corsa Performance, CV4, Dart Machinery, Diamond Pistons, Haltech, JE Pistons, K1 Technologies, Manley, MGP Connecting Rods, PAC Racing Valve Springs, ProX, Rekluse, RevMax, TransGo, Transmission Specialties, Trend Performance, Victory1, Volant Performance and Wiseco. RWB sells to a diverse and unmatched customer base of professional and sportsman racers, engine builders, enthusiasts, niche OEMs, dealers and wholesale distributors. With its differentiated leadership value proposition, RWB has established broad coverage across both automotive and powersports applications and channels. RWB is headquartered in Mentor, Ohio with primary sales offices and manufacturing operations in strategic locations throughout the United States, Canada, Australia and Europe, and other supporting locations around the world.
